    var DataLayerClientPopulator = (function() {
        var self = {};
        
        // Fill datalayer with client side informations
        self.fillDataLayer = function() {
            // Set Do not Track field
            setDoNotTrack();
        };
        
        // Set the Do Not Track information from the navigator.
        function setDoNotTrack() {
            if (navigator.doNotTrack) {
                tc_vars.env_dnt = navigator.doNotTrack;
            } else {
                tc_vars.env_dnt = "0";
            }
        }
        
        return self;
    })();

    // Fill DataLayer with datas retrieved from the client
    DataLayerClientPopulator.fillDataLayer();

</div><div class="recall-slide recall-slide-active">
<div class="row">
<div class="col-sm-2">
<img alt="" class="img-responsive" src="/static/2019/LP/services/rappels-produits/2019/lampe-frontale.jpg"/>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-10">
<div class="content">
<h3 class="title-p">Lampe frontale OnNight 410 - Kalenji</h3>
<p class="recall-body">
<br/>Dans le cadre de nos processus qualité, les équipes de Kalenji et Geonaute ont identifié un risque potentiel: La batterie risque de surchauffer pendant la recharge et peut exposer les utilisateurs à un risque d'incendie / de brûlure.</p>
<a class="cta-v2 cta-v2--alt" href="/static/2019/LP/services/rappels-produits/2019/rappel-produit-OnNight410.pdf" target="_blank">En savoir +</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!--Product 2-->
<div class="recall-slide recall-slide-active">
<div class="row">
<div class="col-sm-2">
<img alt="" class="img-responsive" src="/static/2019/LP/services/rappels-produits/2019/bouee.jpg"/>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-10">
<div class="content">
<h3 class="title-p">Bouée siège bébé - Nabaiji</h3>
<p class="recall-body">
<br/>Dans le cadre de nos processus qualité, les équipes NABAIJI ont identifié un risque potentiel : la jonction entre la bouée et le siège peut se désolidariser.</p>
<a class="cta-v2 cta-v2--alt" href="/static/2019/LP/services/rappels-produits/2019/rappel-produit-bouee.pdf" target="_blank">En savoir +</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!--Product 3-->
<div class="recall-slide recall-slide-active">
<div class="row">
<div class="col-sm-2">
<img alt="" class="img-responsive" src="/static/2019/LP/services/rappels-produits/2019/toucan-simond.jpg"/>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-10">
<div class="content">
<h3 class="title-p">Assureur Toucan Light - Simond</h3>
<p class="recall-body">
<br/>Dans le cadre de nos processus qualité, nous avons identifié un risque potentiel : dans le cas de l’assurage de 2 grimpeurs simultanément depuis le relais, si un des deux grimpeurs se met en tension sur la corde, le second grimpeur est bloqué.</p>
<a class="cta-v2 cta-v2--alt" href="/static/2019/LP/services/rappels-produits/2019/rappel-produit-toucan-simond.pdf" target="_blank">En savoir +</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!--Product 4-->
<div class="recall-slide recall-slide-active">
<div class="row">
<div class="col-sm-2">
<img alt="" class="img-responsive" src="/static/2019/LP/services/rappels-produits/2019/gant-equitation.jpg"/>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-10">
<div class="content">
<h3 class="title-p">Gant équitation Pro Leather - Fouganza</h3>
<p class="recall-body">
<br/>Dans le cadre des démarches qualité et sécurité, les équipes de la marque FOUGANZA ont constaté que certains produits avaient une concentration de chrome 6 trop élevée, ce qui pourrait entraîner, dans de rares cas, des réactions allergiques.</p>
<a class="cta-v2 cta-v2--alt" href="/static/2019/LP/services/rappels-produits/2019/rappel-produit-gant-equitation.pdf" target="_blank">En savoir +</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!--Product 5-->
<div class="recall-slide recall-slide-active">
<div class="row">
<div class="col-sm-2">
<img alt="" class="img-responsive" src="/static/2019/LP/services/rappels-produits/2019/mousqueton.jpg"/>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-10">
<div class="content">
<h3 class="title-p">Mousqueton Rocky - Simond</h3>
<p class="recall-body">
<br/>Dans le cadre de nos processus qualité, les équipes Simond ont identifié un risque potentiel : nous avons détecté que dans de rares cas, la bague de verrouillage (partie noire) du mousqueton pouvait être montée à l’envers. Ceci peut entraîner un risque de déverrouillage imprévisible du mousqueton durant son utilisation.</p>
<a class="cta-v2 cta-v2--alt" href="/static/2019/LP/services/rappels-produits/2019/rappel-produit-mousqueton.pdf" target="_blank">En savoir +</a>
</div>

<script>
        jQuery(document).ready(function() {
            var slides = $('.recall-slide'),
                autoClick;
            slides.first().before(slides.last());
            if($('.recall-slider .recall-slide').length < 2){
                $('.recall-slider-wrapper .recall-btn').hide();
                return;
            }
            if($('.recall-slider .recall-slide').length == 2){
                $('.recall-slide').clone().appendTo('.recall-slider');
                $('.recall-slide-active').last().removeClass('recall-slide-active');
            }
            $('.recall-slider').height($('.recall-slide.recall-slide-active').outerHeight());

            $('.recall-btn').on('click', function() {
                slides = $('.recall-slide');
                var button = $(this);
                var activeSlide = $('.recall-slide-active');
                if(button.attr('id') == 'recall-next') {
                    slides.last().after(slides.first());
                    activeSlide.removeClass('recall-slide-active').next('.recall-slide').addClass('recall-slide-active');
                    $('.recall-slider').height(activeSlide.next('.recall-slide').outerHeight());
                    clearInterval(autoClick);
                    autoClick = setInterval(function(){
                        $('.recall-btn.right').trigger('click');
                    },8000);
                }
                if(button.attr('id') == 'recall-previous') {
                    slides.first().before(slides.last());
                    activeSlide.removeClass('recall-slide-active').prev('.recall-slide').addClass('recall-slide-active');
                    $('.recall-slider').height(activeSlide.prev('.recall-slide').outerHeight());
                    clearInterval(autoClick);
                    autoClick = setInterval(function(){
                        $('.recall-btn.right').trigger('click');
                    },8000);
                }
            });
            if($('.recall-slider .recall-slide').length > 1){
                autoClick = setInterval(function(){
                    $('.recall-btn.right').trigger('click');
                },8000);
            }
        });
        jQuery(window).resize(function() {
            if($('.recall-slider .recall-slide').length > 1){
                $('.recall-slider').height($('.recall-slide.recall-slide-active').outerHeight());
            }
        });
        </script>
</div>

    var DataLayerClientPopulator = (function() {
        var self = {};
        
        // Fill datalayer with client side informations
        self.fillDataLayer = function() {
            // Set Do not Track field
            setDoNotTrack();
        };
        
        // Set the Do Not Track information from the navigator.
        function setDoNotTrack() {
            if (navigator.doNotTrack) {
                tc_vars.env_dnt = navigator.doNotTrack;
            } else {
                tc_vars.env_dnt = "0";
            }
        }
        
        return self;
    })();

    // Fill DataLayer with datas retrieved from the client
    DataLayerClientPopulator.fillDataLayer();
//]]>
</script>
<script src="//prod-js.aws.y-track.com/v5/profile-hub.min.js"></script>
<script>
    var tagCommanderAjaxExternalServiceList = new Array();
    var tagCommanderAjaxExternalServiceCpt = 0;

    //Include GLOBAL container : tc_global_header.js
    var tagCommanderIncludeContainerGlobal = function (_callback) {
        try {
            var script = document.createElement('script');
            script.type = "text/javascript";
            script.src = "/fassets/tc_global_header.js";
            script.async = true;
            if (_callback !== null) {
                script.onload = _callback;
            }
            
                (document.getElementsByTagName('body')[0]).appendChild(script);
            
        } catch(err) {}
    };

    /* Begin Ysance and service configuration */
    var dmpEnabled = false;
    
    dmpEnabled = true;
    
    if (dmpEnabled && (typeof dmpEnabledHeader === "undefined" || (typeof dmpEnabledHeader !== "undefined" && dmpEnabledHeader))) {
    var ytAddDmpVariablesService = function () { 
    	//Begin configuration
    	
        var _dmpID = tc_vars["user_id_shared"];
        
        
        var ytAccountId = "YT-000066-1"; //Ysance Account ID
        
        
        var ytDomainId = "0021"; //Ysance Domain ID
        
        
        var ytDmpKeys = "all"; //Ysance get all fields
        
		
        var ytDmpKeysException = "segments,method"; //Ysance fields exclusion
        
        
        var ytDmpToken = ""; //Ysance token
        
        
        var ytDmpPMSrc = "sharedid"; //Ysance PM src
        
        
        var ytCallTimeout = 1000; //Ysance timeout in milliseconds
        
		
        var ytDmpKeySegments = "segments"; //Ysance key segments
        
		
        var ytDmpKeyRecommendations = ""; //Ysance key recommendations
        
        
        var ytDmpSessionReset = true; //Ysance Dmp session storage reset
        var ytSessionStorageTtl = 1800; //Ysance session storage life in seconds
        //End configuration

        try {
            //Your tag variables
            var criterias = {'fields': ytDmpKeys},
                token = ytDmpToken;
            
            /**
            * SessionStorageStrategy
            */
            function SessionStorageStrategy(ttl) {
                this.ttl = ttl || 1800
            }

            SessionStorageStrategy.prototype.setXhr = function(xhr){
                this.xhr = xhr;
            };
        
            SessionStorageStrategy.prototype.call = function (opts) {
                // If session storage is not available, do AJAX call
                if (!sessionStorage) {
                    return this.xhr.call(opts);
                }
        
                var sessionStorageKey = 'YT-' + JSON.stringify(opts.data); // WARNING : You might hash this key
        
                // Retrieve saved information
                var savedVisitorInformation = sessionStorage.getItem(sessionStorageKey);
                if (savedVisitorInformation) {
                    savedVisitorInformation = JSON.parse(savedVisitorInformation);
                }
                if (savedVisitorInformation && savedVisitorInformation.expired_at && savedVisitorInformation.expired_at>(new Date).getTime()) {
                    //If available parse and return
                    if (ytDmpSessionReset) {
                        sessionStorage.setItem(sessionStorageKey, JSON.stringify({data: savedVisitorInformation.data, expired_at: (new Date).getTime()+(this.ttl*1000)})); 
                    }
                    opts.cbSuccess(savedVisitorInformation.data);
                    return null;
                } else {
                    //If not available, override success to save back in sessionStorage
                    var cbSuccessOrigin = opts.cbSuccess;
                    opts.cbSuccess = (function(data) {
                        sessionStorage.setItem(sessionStorageKey, JSON.stringify({data: data, expired_at: (new Date).getTime()+(this.ttl*1000)}));
                        return cbSuccessOrigin(data);
                    }).bind(this);
                    //Do AJAX call
                    return this.xhr.call(opts);
                }
            };

            //Profile match retrieval
            if (_dmpID !== undefined && _dmpID !== "") {
                criterias.pm_id = _dmpID;
                criterias.pm_src = ytDmpPMSrc;
            }
            
            //From here you have the code which interact with the live api
            var api = new YtApi({
                accountId: ytAccountId,
                domainId: ytDomainId,
                strategy: new SessionStorageStrategy(ytSessionStorageTtl),
                token: token
            });

            var xhrAnswered = false;
            
            var xhr = api.findCurrentVisitor(
                criterias,
                function (data) {
                    window.ytVisitorInformation = data;
                    xhrAnswered = true;
                    var setPersoUpdate = 0;
                    Object.keys(data).map(function(item){
                        if (ytDmpKeysException.indexOf(item) < 0) {
                            tc_vars["dmp_user_"+item] = data[item];
                            setPersoUpdate++;
                        } else if(item === ytDmpKeySegments) {
                            var segments = data[item];
                            for (segmentIdx = 0; segmentIdx < segments.length; segmentIdx++) {
                                tc_vars["dmp_segment" + (segmentIdx + 1)] = segments[segmentIdx];
                                setPersoUpdate++;
                            }
                        }
                    });
                    
                    tagCommanderAjaxExternalServiceCpt++;
                    tagCommanderIncludeContainerGlobal(function() {
                        if (setPersoUpdate > 0 && PERSOFramework) {
                            //Force to update profile customer on server
                            PERSOFramework.setLocalStorage();
                        }
                    });
                },
                function (e) {
                    xhrAnswered = true;
                    tagCommanderAjaxExternalServiceCpt++;
                    tagCommanderIncludeContainerGlobal(null);
                }
            );

            //Add a timeout to the request
            setTimeout(
                function () {
                    if (!xhrAnswered) {
                        console.debug('api.findCurrentVisitor : timeout');
                        if (xhr) {
                            xhr.abort();
                        }
                        tagCommanderAjaxExternalServiceCpt++;
                        tagCommanderIncludeContainerGlobal(null);
                    }
                },
                ytCallTimeout
            ); 
        } catch(err) {
            console.debug(err);
            tagCommanderAjaxExternalServiceCpt++;
            tagCommanderIncludeContainerGlobal(null);
        }
    }
    tagCommanderAjaxExternalServiceList.push(ytAddDmpVariablesService);
    /* End Ysance and service configuration */
	} else {
    tagCommanderIncludeContainerGlobal(null);
	}
	
    //Call all the services added to tagCommanderAjaxExternalServiceList
    for (i = 0; i < tagCommanderAjaxExternalServiceList.length; i++) { 
        tagCommanderAjaxExternalServiceList[i]();
    }


    </script>
<!-- TagCommander -->
